City Official Website
Lafayette is a city in Boulder County, Colorado, incorporated on January 6, 1890. 

Website: https://www.lafayetteco.gov
City Hall Address: 451 N 111th Street, Lafayette, CO 80026
Phone: (303) 665-5588
Hours: Mon–Fri, 8:00 AM – 5:00 PM
